{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_018EpnTVj3jbb6KyVR5VoiNm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01YAZVCYAijv7t4Vags1NQjv","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/patches/SECURITY_FIX_SUMMARY.md","content":"# Apache Druid 0.20.0 JavaScript Execution Vulnerability - Security Fix\n\n## Vulnerability Description\n\nApache Druid version 0.20.0 contains a critical remote code execution vulnerability in the JavaScript filter functionality. Authenticated attackers can execute arbitrary Java code on the server by providing malicious JavaScript payloads through the sampler endpoint (`/druid/indexer/v1/sampler`).\n\n### Attack Vector\n\nThe vulnerability exists in the `JavaScriptDimFilter` class which uses the Mozilla Rhino JavaScript engine without any sandboxing or security restrictions. The Rhino engine allows JavaScript code to access Java classes through patterns like:\n\n```javascript\njava.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec('malicious_command')\n```\n\nAn attacker can exploit this by sending a POST request to the sampler endpoint with a malicious JavaScript filter:\n\n```http\nPOST /druid/indexer/v1/sampler HTTP/1.1\nContent-Type: application/json\n\n{\n  \"type\": \"index\",\n  \"spec\": {\n    \"dataSchema\": {\n      \"transformSpec\": {\n        \"filter\": {\n          \"type\": \"javascript\",\n          \"function\": \"function(){java.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec('malicious_command');}\",\n          \"\": {\n            \"enabled\": true\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\nThe empty key `\"\"` in the JSON payload can bypass certain validation logic.\n\n## Root Cause\n\nThe vulnerability stems from multiple security gaps:\n\n1. **No Sandboxing**: The Mozilla Rhino JavaScript engine is initialized without a `ClassShutter`, allowing full access to Java classes.\n2. **No Input Validation**: JavaScript function strings are accepted directly from JSON input without validation.\n3. **No Pattern Checking**: The code doesn't check for dangerous patterns like Java class access, eval, or Function constructors.\n\n## Security Fix\n\nThe patch implements three layers of defense:\n\n### 1. Input Validation (validateJavaScriptFunction)\n\n- Validates that the function doesn't contain dangerous patterns:\n  - Java class access patterns: `java.`, `Java.`, `Packages`\n  - Dynamic code execution: `eval`, `Function`\n- Validates function syntax to ensure it's a properly formed JavaScript function\n\n### 2. Function Syntax Validation (validateFunctionSyntax)\n\n- Ensures functions start with the `function` keyword\n- Checks for proper parentheses and curly braces\n- Prevents obfuscated or non-function code\n\n### 3. Rhino Engine Security (RestrictedClassShutter)\n\n- Implements the `ClassShutter` interface to restrict Java class access\n- Blocks all `java.*`, `javax.*`, and `com.sun.*` packages\n- Only allows safe JavaScript built-in objects\n\n## Implementation Details\n\n### Modified File\n- `/root/druid/processing/src/main/java/org/apache/druid/query/filter/JavaScriptDimFilter.java`\n\n### Changes Made\n\n1. **Added ClassShutter Import**\n   ```java\n   import org.mozilla.javascript.ClassShutter;\n   ```\n\n2. **Enhanced Constructor Validation**\n   - Authenticated attackers can execute arbitrary Java code on the server by providing malicious JavaScript payloads through the sampler endpoint (`/druid/indexer/v1/sampler`).\n\n### Attack Vector\n\nThe vulnerability exists in the `JavaScriptDimFilter` class which uses the Mozilla Rhino JavaScript engine without any sandboxing or security restrictions. The Rhino engine allows JavaScript code to access Java classes through patterns like:\n\n```javascript\njava.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec('malicious_command')\n```\n\nAn attacker can exploit this by sending a POST request to the sampler endpoint with a malicious JavaScript filter:\n\n```http\nPOST /druid/indexer/v1/sampler HTTP/1.1\nContent-Type: application/json\n\n{\n  \"type\": \"index\",\n  \"spec\": {\n    \"dataSchema\": {\n      \"transformSpec\": {\n        \"filter\": {\n          \"type\": \"javascript\",\n          \"function\": \"function(){java.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec('malicious_command');}\",\n          \"\": {\n            \"enabled\": true\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\nThe empty key `\"\"` in the JSON payload can bypass certain validation logic.\n\n## Root Cause\n\nThe vulnerability stems from multiple security gaps:\n\n1. **No Sandboxing**: The Mozilla Rhino JavaScript engine is initialized without a `ClassShutter`, allowing full access to Java classes.\n2. **No Input Validation**: JavaScript function strings are accepted directly from JSON input without validation.\n3. **No Pattern Checking**: The code doesn't check for dangerous patterns like Java class access, eval, or Function constructors.\n\n## Security Fix\n\nThe patch implements three layers of defense:\n\n### 1. Input Validation (validateJavaScriptFunction)\n\n- Validates that the function doesn't contain dangerous patterns:\n  - Java class access patterns: `java.`, `Java.`, `Packages`\n  - Dynamic code execution: `eval`, `Function`\n- Validates function syntax to ensure it's a properly formed JavaScript function\n\n### 2. Function Syntax Validation (validateFunctionSyntax)\n\n- Ensures functions start with the `function` keyword\n- Checks for proper parentheses and curly braces\n- Prevents obfuscated or non-function code\n\n### 3. Rhino Engine Security (RestrictedClassShutter)\n\n- Implements the `ClassShutter` interface to restrict Java class access\n- Blocks all `java.*`, `javax.*`, and `com.sun.*` packages\n- Only allows safe JavaScript built-in objects\n\n## Implementation Details\n\n### Modified File\n- `/root/druid/processing/src/main/java/org/apache/druid/query/filter/JavaScriptDimFilter.java`\n\n### Changes Made\n\n1. **Added ClassShutter Import**\n   ```java\n   import org.mozilla.javascript.ClassShutter;\n   ```\n\n2. **Enhanced Constructor Validation**\n   - Added checks for empty dimension and function strings\n   - Added call to `validateJavaScriptFunction()` in the `@JsonCreator` constructor\n\n3. **Added Security Methods**\n   - `validateJavaScriptFunction(String)`: Checks for dangerous patterns\n   - `validateFunctionSyntax(String)`: Validates JavaScript syntax\n   - `RestrictedClassShutter`: Inner class implementing ClassShutter\n\n4. **Updated JavaScriptPredicateFactory Initialization**\n   - Set ClassShutter on the Rhino Context: `cx.setClassShutter(new RestrictedClassShutter())`\n   - This prevents JavaScript code from accessing Java classes\n\n## Testing\n\nThe fix ensures that:\n\n1. **Exploit requests are blocked**: Any attempt to access Java classes or use dangerous patterns will result in an `IllegalArgumentException`\n2. **Legitimate requests work**: Valid JavaScript functions that don't contain dangerous patterns continue to work normally\n3. **Defense in depth**: Multiple validation layers prevent various attack vectors\n\n### Test Cases\n\nBlocked patterns:\n- `\"function(){java.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec('cmd');}\"`\n- `\"function(){Java.type('java.lang.Runtime').getRuntime().exec('cmd');}\"`\n- `\"function(){eval('malicious code');}\"`\n- `\"function(){new Function('malicious code')()}\"`\n- Invalid syntax like `\"\"` (empty string key)\n\nAllowed patterns:\n- `\"function(x){return x > 5;}\"`\n- `\"function(x){return x.match(/pattern/);}\"`\n- `\"function(x){var result = x; return result;}\"`\n\n## Build Instructions\n\nThe patched Druid has been built with Maven, excluding web-console and code quality checks:\n\n```bash\ncd /root/druid\nmvn clean package -DskipTests -Dcheckstyle.skip=true -Dpmd.skip=true \\\n  -Dforbiddenapis.skip=true -Dspotbugs.skip=true -Danimal.sniffer.skip=true \\\n  -Denforcer.skip=true -Djacoco.skip=true -Ddependency-check.skip=true \\\n  -pl '!web-console' -pl indexing-service -am\n```\n\nThe patched JAR file is located at:\n- `/root/druid/indexing-service/target/druid-indexing-service-0.20.0.jar`\n\n## Deployment\n\nThe patched JAR should be deployed to `/opt/druid/lib/` and the Druid server restarted:\n\n```bash\ncp /root/druid/indexing-service/target/druid-indexing-service-0.20.0.jar /opt/druid/lib/\nsystemctl restart druid\n```\n\n## Verification\n\nAfter deployment, verify the fix by attempting the exploit request. The vulnerability allows authenticated attackers to execute arbitrary Java code through malicious JavaScript payloads in the sampler endpoint.\n\n## Vulnerability Details\n\n**CVE Type**: Remote Code Execution (RCE)\n**Affected Component**: JavaScript Filter (`JavaScriptDimFilter`)\n**Affected Endpoint**: `/druid/indexer/v1/sampler`\n**Severity**: Critical\n**Authentication Required**: Yes (authenticated users)\n\n### Attack Mechanism\n\nThe Mozilla Rhino JavaScript engine used by Druid's JavaScript filter does not restrict access to Java classes. Attackers can leverage this to:\n\n1. Access `java.lang.Runtime` class\n2. Execute system commands via `Runtime.getRuntime().exec()`\n3. Access file systems, network services, and other system resources\n\n### Example Exploit\n\n```http\nPOST /druid/indexer/v1/sampler HTTP/1.1\nContent-Type: application/json\n\n{\n  \"type\": \"index\",\n  \"spec\": {\n    \"dataSchema\": {\n      \"transformSpec\": {\n        \"filter\": {\n          \"type\": \"javascript\",\n          \"function\": \"function(){java.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec('whoami');}\",\n          \"\": {\"enabled\": true}\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Patch Files\n\n### 0001-security-fix.patch (Primary Fix)\nThe main security patch that implements all necessary protections. This is the most important patch.\n\n**Changes**:\n- Adds `ClassShutter` import for Rhino engine security\n- Implements `RestrictedClassShutter` class to block Java class access\n- Adds `validateJavaScriptFunction()` for input validation\n- Adds `validateFunctionSyntax()` for syntax validation\n- Enhances constructor with empty value checks\n- Integrates ClassShutter into Rhino context initialization\n\n### 0001-Add-JavaScript-security-sandboxing-with-ClassShutter.patch\nFocused patch for Rhino ClassShutter implementation.\n\n### 0002-Validate-JavaScript-filter-properties-and-block-empty-keys.patch\nInput validation and syntax checking patch.\n\n### 0003-Add-JSON-validation-for-filter-objects.patch\nJSON object validation patch (skeleton).\n\n## Security Layers\n\nThe patch implements three layers of defense:\n\n### Layer 1: Input Validation\n```java\n// Blocks dangerous patterns\n- Java class access: java.*, Java.*, Packages\n- Dynamic execution: eval, Function\n- Empty values in filter properties\n```\n\n### Layer 2: Syntax Validation\n```java\n// Enforces proper JavaScript syntax\n- Must start with \"function\" keyword\n- Must have parentheses ()\n- Must have curly braces {}\n```\n\n### Layer 3: Runtime Sandboxing\n```java\n// ClassShutter blocks Java access at runtime\n- Blocks: java.*, javax.*, com.sun.*\n- Allows: Only safe JavaScript built-ins\n- Prevents: Java interop from JavaScript\n```\n\n## Installation Instructions\n\n### Prerequisites\n- Apache Druid 0.20.0 source code at The vulnerability allows authenticated attackers to execute arbitrary Java code through malicious JavaScript payloads in the sampler endpoint.\n\n## Vulnerability Details\n\n**CVE Type**: Remote Code Execution (RCE)\n**Affected Component**: JavaScript Filter (`JavaScriptDimFilter`)\n**Affected Endpoint**: `/druid/indexer/v1/sampler`\n**Severity**: Critical\n**Authentication Required**: Yes (authenticated users)\n\n### Attack Mechanism\n\nThe Mozilla Rhino JavaScript engine used by Druid's JavaScript filter does not restrict access to Java classes. Attackers can leverage this to:\n\n1. Access `java.lang.Runtime` class\n2. Execute system commands via `Runtime.getRuntime().exec()`\n3. Access file systems, network services, and other system resources\n\n### Example Exploit\n\n```http\nPOST /druid/indexer/v1/sampler HTTP/1.1\nContent-Type: application/json\n\n{\n  \"type\": \"index\",\n  \"spec\": {\n    \"dataSchema\": {\n      \"transformSpec\": {\n        \"filter\": {\n          \"type\": \"javascript\",\n          \"function\": \"function(){java.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec('whoami');}\",\n          \"\": {\"enabled\": true}\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Patch Files\n\n### 0001-security-fix.patch (Primary Fix)\nThe main security patch that implements all necessary protections. This is the most important patch.\n\n**Changes**:\n- Adds `ClassShutter` import for Rhino engine security\n- Implements `RestrictedClassShutter` class to block Java class access\n- Adds `validateJavaScriptFunction()` for input validation\n- Adds `validateFunctionSyntax()` for syntax validation\n- Enhances constructor with empty value checks\n- Integrates ClassShutter into Rhino context initialization\n\n### 0001-Add-JavaScript-security-sandboxing-with-ClassShutter.patch\nFocused patch for Rhino ClassShutter implementation.\n\n### 0002-Validate-JavaScript-filter-properties-and-block-empty-keys.patch\nInput validation and syntax checking patch.\n\n### 0003-Add-JSON-validation-for-filter-objects.patch\nJSON object validation patch (skeleton).\n\n## Security Layers\n\nThe patch implements three layers of defense:\n\n### Layer 1: Input Validation\n```java\n// Blocks dangerous patterns\n- Java class access: java.*, Java.*, Packages\n- Dynamic execution: eval, Function\n- Empty values in filter properties\n```\n\n### Layer 2: Syntax Validation\n```java\n// Enforces proper JavaScript syntax\n- Must start with \"function\" keyword\n- Must have parentheses ()\n- Must have curly braces {}\n```\n\n### Layer 3: Runtime Sandboxing\n```java\n// ClassShutter blocks Java access at runtime\n- Blocks: java.*, javax.*, com.sun.*\n- Allows: Only safe JavaScript built-ins\n- Prevents: Java interop from JavaScript\n```\n\n## Installation Instructions\n\n### Prerequisites\n- Apache Druid 0.20.0 source code at `/root/druid`\n- Maven 3.6+\n- Java 8+\n\n### Step 1: Apply Patches\n```bash\ncd /root/druid\n# The patches have been manually applied to the source code\n```\n\n### Step 2: Build Druid\n```bash\nmvn clean package -DskipTests -Dcheckstyle.skip=true -Dpmd.skip=true \\\n  -Dforbiddenapis.skip=true -Dspotbugs.skip=true -Danimal.sniffer.skip=true \\\n  -Denforcer.skip=true -Djacoco.skip=true -Ddependency-check.skip=true \\\n  -pl '!web-console' -pl indexing-service -am\n```\n\n### Step 3: Deploy\n```bash\n# Copy the patched JAR to Druid installation\ncp /root/druid/indexing-service/target/druid-indexing-service-0.20.0.jar \\\n   /opt/druid/lib/\n\n# Restart Druid\nsystemctl restart druid\n```\n\n## Verification\n\n### Test 1: Block Java Access\n```bash\ncurl -X POST http://druid-server:8090/druid/indexer/v1/sampler \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\n    \"type\": \"index\",\n    \"spec\": {\n      \"dataSchema\": {\n        \"transformSpec\": {\n          \"filter\": {\n            \"type\": \"javascript\",\n            \"function\": \"function(){java.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec(\\\"whoami\\\");}\"\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }'\n```\n**Expected Result**: `IllegalArgumentException` - JavaScript functions cannot access Java classes\n\n### Test 2: Allow Legitimate JavaScript\n```bash\ncurl -X POST http://druid-server:8090/druid/indexer/v1/sampler \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\n    \"type\": \"index\",\n    \"spec\": {\n      \"dataSchema\": {\n        \"transformSpec\": {\n          \"filter\": {\n            \"type\": \"javascript\",\n            \"function\": \"function(x){return x > 5;}\"\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }'\n```\n**Expected Result**: Success - filter works normally\n\n### Test 3: Block eval()\n```bash\ncurl -X POST dynamic code execution\n- Bypass security checks\n\n## Testing Results\n\nBuild Status: ✅ **SUCCESS**\n- druid-core: SUCCESS\n- druid-processing: SUCCESS (with patched JavaScriptDimFilter)\n-
